# Gig: Treating Symptoms

Journal: `ep1/quests/street_stories/sts_ep1_12` · `StreetStory` · hash `1043266227`.

Journal premise: Gig objective: Eliminate Milko Alexis\n\nLocation: Voodoo Boys base, Luxor High Wellness Spa\n\nDetails: Indira Barazza wouldn’t be the first or the last to fall prey to the Voodoo Boys’ blackmail. What’s interesting is the gang seems to have only the technical employees of her corp in their sights. They made our client an offer she couldn’t refuse – cooperate or die. Indira chose a third route: she called me. The Voodoo Boy threatening to hack her lungs is one Milko Alexis. See to it that he kindly leaves her the fuck alone.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 81 exact journal-linked nodes in 5 resources; 12 resources in the inspected dependency context.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

619 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 77 distinct direct resource identities; 29 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
